# Current comparison — Devices corrective Step 1, 17 November 2026

477 information rows; 39 existing Devices cell corrections and one specialized update-key lifecycle candidate. All 476 starting Devices cells accounted for using retained evidence and targeted original-source review. Twelve Devices source limits remain. PA-DIM Step 2 is next; its new-row comparison is explicitly pending. Color marks potential information need, not exact correspondence, exclusivity or adoption.
